I-35 Caps, Tax Relief, Solar Power, & Park Name Change

Thursday, May 22, 2025 Austin City Council Regular Meeting

Here's what's happening at the Austin City Council meeting:

  • Major I-35 Project Decisions:

    The Council will deliberate on critical next steps for the I-35 capping projects, including funding and design, a highly anticipated discussion with multiple proposed compromises.

  • Tax Relief and Youth Protection:

    Residents will see a property tax exemption increase to $192,000 for seniors and disabled individuals. New policies are also set to create vape-free zones around schools to safeguard youth health.

  • Boosting Green Energy & Efficiency:

    A new initiative will launch to explore installing solar panels on city-owned facilities, aiming for cost savings, job creation, and carbon reduction. The Council also seeks more inter-local government cooperation to streamline services.

  • Community and Accountability:

    Weston Field Park is set to be renamed in honor of baseball legend and civil rights figure Don Baylor. Separately, a $4.5 million settlement was approved for an individual injured by police during 2020 protests.

VAPE STORES FEATURE PRODUCTS WITH BRIGHT COLORS, CARTOON CHARACTERS AND FUN FLAVORS, DELIBERATELY CRAFTED TO CATCH THE EYES OF YOUTH. AND THESE PRODUCTS ARE OFTEN ADDICTIVE AND INTOXICATING. OTHER MARKETING TACTICS LIKE USING SUPERHEROES, CELEBRITIES, AND GLAMOROUS DEPICTIONS OF USE GIVE YOUNG PEOPLE THE FALSE IMPRESSION THAT THESE PRODUCTS ARE HARMLESS. THERE ARE VERY FEW LIMITATIONS ON MARKETING AND SALES OF E-CIGARETTES COMPARED TO THE RESTRICTIONS WE HAVE ON REGULAR CIGARETTES. WHEEN ON BEHALF OF THE APPLICANT, OBVIOUSLY I APPRECIATE THIS IS A DIFFICULT CASE AND THAT THE FOCUS APPROPRIATELY HAS BEEN ON HOUSING POLICY CHALLENGES THAT THIS CASE HAS ELEVATED. I WANTED TO HIGHLIGHT THREE OF THE MAJOR CHALLENGES FACING THE CITY TO MEETING HOUSING GOALS, ESPECIALLY IN THIS DISTRICT. ONE, THE LACK OF AN EXISTING HOUSING PIPELINE THAT IS THE LACK OF NEW HOUSING THAT CAN HELP MEET CURRENT DEMAND AND AGE ULTIMATELY INTO NATURAL AFFORDABILITY. TWO, THE LACK OF AREAS DESIGNATED FOR HOUSING GROWTH SUCH AS IMAGINE AUSTIN CENTERS. AND THREE, THE LACK OF PROPERTY ZONED FOR MULTIFAMILY AND AFFORDABILITY DENSITY BONUSES. JANE JACOBS IN HER OFTEN CITED BOOK, THE DEATH AND LIFE OF THE GREAT AMERICAN CITIES, WHICH HAS BEEN REFERRED TO AS A FOUNDATIONAL TEXT OF HUMANE URBAN PLANNING, DESCRIBE THE NEED FOR HOUSING PIPELINE. NOTING THAT QUOTE, SOME OF THE OLD BUILDINGS YEAR BY YEAR ARE REPLACED BY NEW ONES OR REHABILITATED TO A DEGREE EQUIVALENT TO REPLACEMENT END QUOTE. AND WITH QUOTE, WHAT WAS ONCE NEW IN THE MIXTURE OF EVENTUALLY BECOMING WHAT IS OLD IN THE HOUSING MIXTURE. THIS HELPS CREATE A CONTINUOUS PIPELINE OF HOUSING, OF DIFFERENT AGES TO MEET HOUSING DEMAND, EVEN AS SOME PROPERTIES REACH THEIR END OF LIFE. AT THE SAME TIME, THIS PROCESS CAN ALSO CREATE TENSION AS NEW UNITS WILL NECESSARILY NEED TO COVER, UH, THE COST OF CONSTRUCTION TO HELP SOLVE FOR THIS ISSUE, THE CITY HAS ESTABLISHED DENSITY BONUS PROGRAMS LIKE DB 90 TO MAKE A DOWN PAYMENT ON AFFORDABILITY. IN TEXAS, PROPERTIES ALWAYS HAVE THE OPTION TO REDEVELOP UNDER THEIR BASE ENTITLEMENTS, BUT CITIES CAN ENCOURAGE AFFORDABILITY THROUGH DENSITY BONUS PROGRAMS, WHICH LEVERAGE EXISTING MARKET PRESSURES TO ENSURE THAT A PORTION OF NEW UNITS ARE AFFORDABLE ON DAY ONE. FOR PROPERTIES THAT HAVE REACHED END OF LIFE, THE ALTERNATIVE IS OFTEN EITHER A FULLY MARKET RATE PROJECT OR A DENSITY BONUS PROJECT WITH AFFORDABLE UNITS ON DAY ONE. THESE DENSITY UNIT, THESE DENSITY PROGRAMS, UH, ALSO PROVIDE MEANINGFUL TENANT ASSISTANCE THAT THE CITY CANNOT OTHERWISE REQUIRE UNDER THE BASE ZONING. IN ADDITION TO THE REQUIRED TENANT ASSISTANCE, THE PROPERTY OWNER WILL HELP TENANTS IDENTIFY HOUSING ACCOMMODATIONS IN THIS CASE. WE ALSO HAVE THE ABILITY TO MITIGATE IMPACTS THROUGH THE APPROXIMATELY 2,293 COMPARABLY PRICED VACANCIES THAT EXIST IN SOME OF THE OLDER COMPLEXES IN THE AREA, WHICH COULD HELP RESIDENTS REMAIN IN THIS PARTICULAR NEIGHBORHOOD. I THINK IT IS ALSO IMPORTANT TO KEEP IN MIND THAT THERE IS A NEED TO PLAN FOR GROWTH AND TO REPLENISH THE HOUSING PIPELINE IN DISTRICT 10. IN PARTICULAR, THIS MAP SHOWS APPROVED DB 90 CASES AS PURPLE PINPOINTS THROUGHOUT THE CITY. TO DATE, THERE HAVE BEEN TWO DB 90 CASES IN DISTRICT 10 THAT'S SHOWN IN BLUE, THAT'S DISTRICT 10, ONE OF WHICH IS WEST OF MOPAC. THE OTHER ONE IS JUST 0.27 ACRES AND ONLY 12 UNITS ON THE EAST SIDE OF MOPAC. ONE OF THE CHALLENGES IS THAT CITY POLICIES GENERALLY DIRECT GROWTH TOWARD [07:40:01] IMAGINE AUSTIN CENTERS, YET THERE ARE A FEW SUCH AREAS DESIGNATED IN DISTRICT 10 TO DATE FOCUSING PRESSURE ON THESE MORE LIMITED LOCATIONS. THIS CASE SITS IN THE ONLY GROWTH AREA THAT IS COMPLETELY WITHIN DISTRICT 10, WHICH IS SHOWN IN THE START AREA ON THE MAP. OVER THE LAST YEAR, THE AUSTIN CITY COUNCIL HAS DELIBERATELY PASSED DENSITY BONUS PROGRAMS, KNOWING THAT THEY WOULD RESULT IN THE LOSS OF AFFORDABLE UNITS AND DISPLACEMENT OF AUSTIN RESIDENTS. POSTPONE ITEM 70. SO YOU CAN MAKE THIS RIGHT FOR THE DENSITY BONUS DB 90 PROGRAM. THE STAFF INCLUDED REQUIREMENTS THAT REMOVED INCENTIVES TO REDEVELOP PROPERTIES WITH NATURALLY AFFORDING, NATURALLY OCCURRING AFFORDABLE HOUSING AND TO REPLACE THE AFFORDABLE UNITS WHEN SUCH REDEVELOPMENT OCCURRED. THESE REQUIREMENTS WERE CONSISTENT WITH THE CITY'S DISPLACEMENT MITIGATION STRATEGY AND STRATEGIC HOUSING BLUEPRINT. HOWEVER, WHEN DB 90 WAS PASSED IN FEBRUARY, 2024, THESE REQUIREMENTS WERE REMOVED. SIX MONTHS LATER, DB 90 WAS REVISED AND TENANT BENEFITS WERE ADDED WHEN REDEVELOPMENT RESULTED IN DEMOLITION. HOWEVER, THE CITY COUNCIL VOTED AGAIN IN AUGUST TO SPECIFICALLY NOT INCLUDE REQUIREMENTS TO REPLACE EXISTING AFFORDABLE HOUSING. WHEN DBE TODD PASSED IN MAY, 2024, THE REQUIREMENT TO REPLACE EXISTING AFFORDABLE HOUSING WAS INCLUDED, BUT THE NUMBER WAS LIMITED TO AT MOST, TWICE THE MINIMUM AND A NEW PROVISION WAS ADDED ALLOWING THE CITY COUNCIL TO WAIVE THE REPLACEMENT REQUIREMENT ENTIRELY. THESE REPEATED DECISIONS OF AUSTIN CITY COUNCIL DEMONSTRATE THE HYPOCRISY OF THE MAYOR AND SEVEN COUNCIL MEMBERS WHO WERE ON THE COUNCIL IN 2024. YOU CREATED AFFORDABILITY BONUS PROGRAMS, BUT DESIGNED THESE PROGRAMS TO RESULT IN A NET LOSS OF AFFORDABLE HOUSING. THANK YOU, MR. GREENBERG. PLEASE FIX IT. NEXT.
